# Spec-collision ratchet baseline.
#
# Regenerate the body below this header with:
#   BOARD_SPEC_BASELINE_DUMP=1 ./.board/validate.sh > /tmp/new-baseline-body.txt
# then replace everything below this comment block with that file's contents
# (keep this header). Never hand-edit a line in to "make the build pass".
#
# Each line is "<field> <id1> <id2>" -- a pairwise verbatim spec collision
# that exists on the board today and is tolerated as known debt. `id1` and
# `id2` are byte-sorted. `<field>` is one of: given, when, then, green,
# non_goal, and, non_goal_extra (Given/When/Then/And of an AC scenario are
# suffixed /AC-NNN; `and` entries are position-scoped so And#1 only competes
# with another card's And#1; `non_goal_extra` entries are position-scoped the
# same way for Non-goal bullets 2+).
#
# The ratchet only shrinks: .board/validate.sh fails if the current tree has
# a pairwise collision not listed here (new debt), and it also fails if a
# line here no longer collides in the current tree (stale/dead entry -- the
# card's text changed and this baseline was not regenerated). Never add an
# entry by hand to "make the build pass"; only regenerate from measured
# reality with the command above, and only after confirming the new debt is
# expected (e.g. from an intentional card edit), not accidental.
